Dùng khi cần biết vấn đề nằm ở RUỘT hay ở KHUNG - tức ở bản thân các yếu tố cấu thành, hay ở cách chúng được tổ chức. Gọi khi đổi người đổi công cụ mà kết quả không đổi, khi có đề xuất tái cấu trúc, hoặc khi cần kiểm yêu cầu bắt buộc về hình thức (hợp đồng, thủ tục, quy trình). ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-noi-dung-hinh-thuc.md))
- **tbc-phan-bien**: Agent đối thủ. Nhiệm vụ duy nhất là ĐẬP kết luận mà các lăng kính khác vừa dựng lên - săn giáo điều, kinh nghiệm chủ nghĩa, tư duy siêu hình, khung bất khả bác bỏ và nguỵ biện nhân quả. Chạy ở pha 4 của skill truy-ban-chat, sau cùng. Không được khen, không được cân bằng - chỉ tìm chỗ sai. ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-phan-bien.md))
- **tbc-phat-trien**: Lăng kính Nguyên lý về sự phát triển - quan điểm phát triển. Dùng khi cần biết đối tượng đang ĐI LÊN hay ĐI XUỐNG, và phân biệt phát triển thật (đổi chất) với tăng trưởng đơn thuần (chỉ to hơn). Gọi khi có ai đó đánh giá một đối tượng bằng trạng thái hiện tại thay vì bằng xu hướng, hoặc khi cần dự báo khuynh hướng. ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-phat-trien.md))
- **tbc-phu-dinh**: Lăng kính Quy luật phủ định của phủ định. Dùng khi cần trả lời "rồi nó sẽ đi về đâu?" — xác định khuynh hướng phát triển, cái mới kế thừa gì từ cái cũ, và cảnh báo phủ định sạch trơn. Gọi khi câu hỏi liên quan tới dự báo xu hướng, chuyển giao, thay thế cái cũ bằng cái mới. ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-phu-dinh.md))
- **tbc-tat-nhien**: Lăng kính Tất nhiên ↔ Ngẫu nhiên. Dùng khi cần phân biệt XU HƯỚNG THẬT với NHIỄU NGẪU NHIÊN — trả lời câu "đây là quy luật hay chỉ là trùng hợp?". Gọi khi có dữ liệu, sự kiện lặp lại, hoặc khi ai đó đang khái quát từ vài ca lẻ thành quy luật. ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-tat-nhien.md))
- **tbc-thuc-tien**: Trọng tài kiểm nghiệm. Nhận các giả thuyết về bản chất/quy luật do những lăng kính khác đưa ra, rồi chỉ ra cách kiểm CỤ THỂ bằng ba hình thức thực tiễn và chấm độ tin cậy xanh/vàng/đỏ. Chạy ở pha 3 của skill truy-ban-chat, sau khi các lăng kính đã cho kết quả. Không có agent này thì mọi kết luận chỉ là suy luận hợp lý, chưa phải chân lý. ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-thuc-tien.md))
- **tbc-toan-dien**: Lăng kính Nguyên lý mối liên hệ phổ biến - quan điểm toàn diện. Dùng khi cần đặt sự việc vào HỆ SINH THÁI của nó thay vì xét biệt lập, và khi cần xếp hạng đâu là mối liên hệ tất yếu, đâu là thứ yếu. Gọi khi ai đó đang cắt rời một sự việc khỏi bối cảnh, hoặc ngược lại đang kể ra quá nhiều yếu tố mà không quyết được cái nào quan trọng. ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-toan-dien.md))
- **tbc-xa-hoi**: Lăng kính Xã hội - Lịch sử. Soi bằng LLSX-QHSX, cơ sở hạ tầng - kiến trúc thượng tầng, tồn tại xã hội - ý thức xã hội, và lợi ích giai cấp. Gọi khi đối tượng là vấn đề xã hội, kinh tế, chính sách, tổ chức, doanh nghiệp, hoặc hành vi con người trong nhóm. Đặc biệt mạnh khi cần trả lời "ai được lợi, ai chịu thiệt, và vì sao người ta nghĩ như vậy". ([haihpse150218/My-second-braind](https://github.com/haihpse150218/My-second-braind/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbc-xa-hoi.md))
- **tbce-author**: Use when creating or editing ThingsBoard CE components — device profiles, devices, dashboards, rule chains, assets, customers. Generates valid JSON ready for import to demo.thingsboard.io. Invoked for requests like "create a device profile", "design a dashboard", "write a rule chain", "add alarm rules", "create an asset hierarchy". ([akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard](https://github.com/akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbce-author.md))
- **tbce-manage**: Use when deploying, importing, exporting, or synchronizing ThingsBoard CE configurations to/from demo.thingsboard.io. Handles authentication, REST API calls, entity listing, and full lifecycle management. Invoked for requests like "import templates", "deploy to ThingsBoard", "list devices", "export dashboard", "delete device profile". ([akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard](https://github.com/akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbce-manage.md))
- **tbce-test**: Use when validating ThingsBoard CE configurations, testing device connectivity, simulating MQTT telemetry, verifying alarm triggers, or checking rule chain logic before deployment. Invoked for requests like "validate this profile", "simulate telemetry", "test the alarm", "check if the rule chain is correct", "send test data". ([akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard](https://github.com/akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbce-test.md))
- **tbce-troubleshoot**: Use when diagnosing ThingsBoard CE issues — device connectivity failures, missing telemetry, rule chain errors, alarm not triggering, dashboard showing no data, or any unexpected behavior. Invoked for requests like "my device is offline", "telemetry not showing", "alarm not firing", "dashboard has no data", "rule chain not working". ([akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard](https://github.com/akashtalole/CC-Plugin-for-Thingsboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbce-troubleshoot.md))
- **tbd-fast**: tbd fast tier at medium reasoning, on claude-opus-5-5, the configured model for this tier. For administrative work (collecting PR and CI state, waiting on CI, bead bookkeeping, posting prepared replies, conflict-free rebases). Spawn it when delegating per tbd shortcut delegate-to-subagents, with a self-contained brief. ([jlevy/tbd](https://github.com/jlevy/tbd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbd-fast.md) · ★95 · 8 copies)
- **tbd-moderate**: tbd moderate tier at xhigh reasoning, on claude-opus-5-5, the configured model for this tier. For addressing findings (code and test edits, confirming fixes, resolving conflicts). Spawn it when delegating per tbd shortcut delegate-to-subagents, with a self-contained brief. ([jlevy/tbd](https://github.com/jlevy/tbd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbd-moderate.md) · ★95 · 8 copies)
- **tbd-strong**: tbd strong tier at xhigh reasoning, on claude-opus-5-5, the configured model for this tier. For senior engineering, security, performance, and correctness reviews, additional review rounds, design decisions, and escalated findings. Spawn it when delegating per tbd shortcut delegate-to-subagents, with a self-contained brief. ([jlevy/tbd](https://github.com/jlevy/tbd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbd-strong.md) · ★95 · 8 copies)
- **tbd-strong-max**: tbd strong tier at max reasoning, on claude-opus-5-5, the configured model for this tier. For the hardest or riskiest strong-tier work among senior engineering, security, performance, and correctness reviews, additional review rounds, design decisions, and escalated findings. Spawn it when delegating per tbd shortcut delegate-to-subagents, with a self-contained brief. ([jlevy/tbd](https://github.com/jlevy/tbd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbd-strong-max.md) · ★95 · 7 copies)
- **tbd-strong-max**: tbd strong tier at max reasoning, on claude-fable-5-1, the configured model for this tier. For the hardest or riskiest strong-tier work among senior engineering, security, performance, and correctness reviews, additional review rounds, design decisions, and escalated findings. Spawn it when delegating per tbd shortcut delegate-to-subagents, with a self-contained brief. ([jlevy/fsqlite](https://github.com/jlevy/fsqlite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tbd-strong-max.md) · ★1)
- **tbdflow-agent**: You are **TBD Flowmaster**, a specialised DevOps agent for enforcing **Trunk-Based Development (TBD)** using the `tbdflow` CLI. ([cladam/tbdflow](https://github.com/cladam/tbdflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/tbdflow-agent.md) · ★63)
- **tbm-vault**: Use on every task. The TBM Knowledge Hub Obsidian vault is the source of truth for TBM Public Adjusters; read it before acting and record results back into it. ([tbmsindemnify/paperclip-jev](https://github.com/tbmsindemnify/paperclip-jev/blob/HEAD/deploy/omarchy/agents/tbm-vault.SKILL.md))
- **tbp-migrate**: You are tasked with migrating legacy Wayfinder issues into the TBP backlog structure using the GitHub CLI. ([vnvalentin/project0](https://github.com/vnvalentin/project0/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/tbp-migrate.md))
- **tbp-wayfinder**: When planning work or mapping a project, you must strictly follow the Toyota Business Practice (TBP) backlog hierarchy. Never use generic "tasks" or "tickets." Work must be structured across 5 levels in GitHub Issues. ([vnvalentin/project0](https://github.com/vnvalentin/project0/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/tbp-wayfinder.md))
- **tc-creator**: Creates one test case in Zephyr or another test-management system from an approved structured spec. Use for bulk TC creation by spawning one independent specialist per TC. ([HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard](https://github.com/HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/tc-creator.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **tc-gen-functional**: 행위 모델로부터 정상·경계·등가분할 기능 TC를 생성할 때 사용. 보안/이상 TC는 별도 에이전트가 담당하므로 위임 금지. ([SmallTyrant/Astartes](https://github.com/SmallTyrant/Astartes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-gen-functional.md))
- **tc-gen-negative**: 권한 거부, 네트워크 단절, 백그라운드/포어그라운드 전환, 탭 전환·새로고침 등 모바일/웹 환경 이상·예외 TC 생성에 자동 위임. ([SmallTyrant/Astartes](https://github.com/SmallTyrant/Astartes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-gen-negative.md))
- **tc-gen-security**: 인증·세션·저장소·네트워크·입력검증·권한 등 범용 보안 TC 생성 전담. OWASP ASVS/MASVS 기반. 보안 시나리오에 자동 위임. ([SmallTyrant/Astartes](https://github.com/SmallTyrant/Astartes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-gen-security.md))
- **tc-generator**: 사전 생성된 TC 뼈대(skeleton)의 빈 칸을 기획서 분석·정답 패턴에 근거해 채워 TC xlsx를 완성하는 에이전트. 임의 행 추가·삭제 금지. Phase 4에서 실행되며 종료 시 scope_manifest_check.py를 반드시 실행한다. ([ellen9417/agentic-tc-harness](https://github.com/ellen9417/agentic-tc-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-generator.md))
- **tc-generator**: 쿼리별 테스트 케이스 생성. converter 완료 후 TC가 필요할 때 위임. 커스텀바인드 → LLM(Bedrock Sonnet, 3 workers 병렬) → merged-tc.json 생성. 반드시 generate-test-cases.py를 사용. ([netsgo0319/oma-claude-code](https://github.com/netsgo0319/oma-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-generator.md))
- **tc-implement**: Implements one QA TC ID as an integration test under packages/rangelink-vscode-extension/src/__integration-tests__/suite/. Traces the production code path, writes the test using helpers/, inserts in TC-ID ascending order, updates the YAML entry's `automated:` field, and runs the matching `pnpm test:release:*` command. ([couimet/rangeLink](https://github.com/couimet/rangeLink/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-implement.md) · ★10)
- **tc-normalizer**: TC JSON을 v3 (시트 형식 통일) 스키마에 맞게 정규화. scripts/normalize_tc.py를 실행하는 thin wrapper. 출력은 outputs/intermediate/tc-reviewed.json (탭 분리 전). ([SmallTyrant/Astartes](https://github.com/SmallTyrant/Astartes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-normalizer.md))
- **tc-platform-splitter**: tc-reviewed.json을 (screen, platform) 탭 단위 파일로 분기. scripts/split_tc.py를 실행하는 thin wrapper. ([SmallTyrant/Astartes](https://github.com/SmallTyrant/Astartes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-platform-splitter.md))
- **tc-reviewer**: Review test cases against ISTQB techniques - coverage items, risk-based depth, TC quality and traceability to AC. Use after gen-testcase or before a test cycle. ([breads8988/claude_pipeline](https://github.com/breads8988/claude_pipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-reviewer.md))
- **tc-reviewer**: Reviewer độc lập cho test case manual của dự án ShopMini. Dùng sau khi thiết kế hoặc sửa test case, để tìm lỗi nghiệp vụ, số tiền sai, thiếu trường hợp biên và câu hỏi BA không cần thiết. Chỉ đọc và báo cáo, không sửa file. ([freelancerhn/manual-test-ai-evolution](https://github.com/freelancerhn/manual-test-ai-evolution/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-reviewer.md))
- **tc-reviewer**: 생성된 TC의 품질·중복·필수 필드를 검증할 때 사용. dedup 모드와 judge 모드가 있으며 호출 시 모드를 명시해야 함. ([SmallTyrant/Astartes](https://github.com/SmallTyrant/Astartes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-reviewer.md))
- **tc-sec**: 联动CWP/KMS/BH/CDS/CFW/SSM/TCSS/WAF/CSIP产品接口生成安全运营报告 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/tc-sec/agents/tc-sec.md) · ★455 · 2 copies)
- **tc-team-designer**: TC 설계 전문가 (tc-team) — 기획서 분석 + MD 파일 2개 생성 + 드라이브 업로드. tc-team S1(run_pipeline_s1only.sh)에서 호출됨. 규칙 단일 소스: rules/tc-분석.md(분석 단계) + rules/tc-설계.md(설계 단계) ([nobles92ts-ship-it/AI_GAME_QA_TestCase](https://github.com/nobles92ts-ship-it/AI_GAME_QA_TestCase/blob/HEAD/agents/tc-team-designer.md) · ★10)
- **tc-team-대조**: MYGAME 뇌 대조 전문가 (tc-team) — analysis.md의 미지정·외부의존 항목을 제2의 뇌(MYGAME 위키 색인)에 대조해 brain_crossref.json을 생성. tc-team S1 체인에서 run_pipeline_s1only.sh가 검수 직전 결정론적으로 호출(crossref_brain=on일 때만). 규칙 단일 소스: rules/tc-대조.md ([nobles92ts-ship-it/AI_GAME_QA_TestCase](https://github.com/nobles92ts-ship-it/AI_GAME_QA_TestCase/blob/HEAD/agents/tc-team-%EB%8C%80%EC%A1%B0.md) · ★10)

([nobles92ts-ship-it/AI_GAME_QA_TestCase](https://github.com/nobles92ts-ship-it/AI_GAME_QA_TestCase/blob/HEAD/agents/tc-team-%EC%84%A4%EA%B3%84%EA%B2%80%EC%88%98.md) · ★10)
- **tc-verifier**: Проверяет тест-кейс SkyPermit по ID (например, API-ANON-004) — находит его в test-cases/, сверяет Steps и Expected result с документацией SkyPermit в Confluence (пространство SPD) и кодом (код только как oracle) и возвращает вердикт «соответствует», «не соответствует» или «документация молчит» со ссылками на разделы. Для кейсов Blocked — OQ-NN сверяет вердикт с реестром test-plans/open-questions.md. Только чтение, ничего не исправляет. Принимает необязательный mode: ui (по умолчанию) | api — в api вместо data-testid сверяет каждый HTTP-шаг с «03 — API Contract». Используй, когда просят провер… ([Wusdor/skypermit-qa-kit-vp](https://github.com/Wusdor/skypermit-qa-kit-vp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-verifier.md))
- **tc-writer**: 테스트 시나리오를 받아 표준 TC 문서(testcase.md)를 생성하고 올바른 폴더에 배치하는 agent ([jhseo808/gwscli-browserharness-automation](https://github.com/jhseo808/gwscli-browserharness-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-writer.md) · ★1)
- **TC-Writer**: /tc-write 스킬이나 사용자가 TC 작성을 요청했을 때만 실행한다. QA플랜/스펙 분석 파일을 읽고 테스트케이스를 작성한다. ([Seolasara/Personal_QA_project_with_AI](https://github.com/Seolasara/Personal_QA_project_with_AI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/TC-Writer.md))
- **tc-writer**: Создаёт ровно один тест-кейс SkyPermit по одному бизнес-правилу с ограничениями и ID, которые передал cover-gaps, и возвращает одну строку JSON. Вызывается из cover-gaps. ([Wusdor/skypermit-qa-kit-vp](https://github.com/Wusdor/skypermit-qa-kit-vp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tc-writer.md))

- **tcell-armorer**: Audits and improves the immune system itself - checks that pass on empty output, speed budgets that never fire, drivers that cannot see what matters, and instruments that are missing. Use every few patrols, or when another immune cell reports "we have no way to measure this". Stem cell: grows into this project's own version on first run. - **tcell-helper**: The one entry point to the immune system. Reads the current state of the immune system, decides which immune cells run and in what order, and owns the consolidated verdict. Use when you are not sure which immune cell you need, or the job spans several. Examples - "is the product healthy", "get me release confidence", "something feels off on Windows", "improve our immune system". ([proxysoul/SoulStack](https://github.com/proxysoul/SoulStack/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/tcell-helper.md) · ★30)

- **tcell-patrol**: Runs the project's immune system (its test suite) across every reachable platform and front door, then sorts every red result into real bug, check bug, environment problem, known, or healed. ([proxysoul/SoulStack](https://github.com/proxysoul/SoulStack/blob/HEAD/agents/tcell-patrol.md) · ★30)

- **tdc-advisory-architecture**: Architecture advisor for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). ANALYZES WITHOUT MODIFYING. Before a structural decision, evaluates how a proposal fits the real Flask + Nuxt 4 BFF + MySQL structure and returns the smallest direct mechanism, at most three alternatives with their consequences, and the decisions only the operator can take. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-advisory-architecture.md))
- **tdc-advisory-coordinator**: Synthesizer of the advisory analyses for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). ANALYZES WITHOUT MODIFYING. Fixes one subject and one falsifiable brief per angle, reconciles the architecture, security, performance and risk analyses the caller supplies against the code, and returns one recommendation plus the closed list of decisions only the operator can take. Never launches agents, never approves. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-advisory-coordinator.md))
- **tdc-advisory-performance**: Performance advisor for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). ANALYZES WITHOUT MODIFYING. Before a decision, locates where a proposal spends — SSR render and cache, Flask workers and DB pool, queries, client bundle and animation — names the instrument and baseline that would decide, and separates recorded targets from proposed ones. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-advisory-performance.md))
- **tdc-advisory-risk**: Risk advisor for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). ANALYZES WITHOUT MODIFYING. Before a risky change — a migration, a deploy or vhost swap, an auth change, a new integration — prints the exact target set, walks each step's failure, detection and leftover state, names the undo or its absence, and frames GO-with-conditions / NO-GO-until / OPERATOR DECISION. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-advisory-risk.md))
- **tdc-advisory-security**: Security advisor for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). ANALYZES WITHOUT MODIFYING. Before a decision, maps the trust boundaries a proposal crosses, states each threat in falsifiable form against the proposal and the status quo, enumerates the whole surface it creates, and names the smallest mitigation with the must-pass and must-refuse evidence to demand. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-advisory-security.md))
- **tdc-api-expert**: HTTP surface specialist for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb) — the Flask /api blueprints, request validation, the {success, data} response envelope, status codes, and how each route is reached from the browser through the Nuxt BFF, routeRules proxies and Apache. Use for creating or changing endpoints; business logic goes to tdc-backend-expert, schema to tdc-database-expert, auth internals to tdc-auth-expert. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-api-expert.md))
- **tdc-auth-expert**: Authentication and authorization specialist for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb) — Flask-issued JWTs, the Nuxt BFF HttpOnly-cookie transport, bcrypt passwords, the user &lt; staff &lt; admin role decorators and client route guards, and the planned OAuth providers. Use for login/refresh/logout, token and cookie handling, role enforcement and new auth features. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-auth-expert.md))
- **tdc-backend-expert**: Flask backend specialist for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb) — the app factory and configuration, models, the db/response/JWT/password utilities, and Python business logic behind the /api blueprints. Use for backend logic and structure; routes go to tdc-api-expert, schema to tdc-database-expert, auth to tdc-auth-expert. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-backend-expert.md))
- **tdc-browser-performance-tester**: Browser performance tester for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). Measures Core Web Vitals, page-load attribution and long-lived animation cost (GSAP ScrollTrigger, Lenis) of the Nuxt 4 site in a real browser — repeated runs, stated conditions, baseline versus candidate — against the targets the project actually records. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-browser-performance-tester.md))
- **tdc-code-reviewer**: The general code reviewer for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb), the general review eye at a canon review point on either harness. ([Alex-65/tdcweb](https://github.com/Alex-65/tdcweb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdc-code-reviewer.md))
- **tdc-database-expert**: MySQL schema, migration and query specialist for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb) — sql/schema.sql and the hand-applied sql/migrations, the mysql-connector-python access layer in backend/app/utils/db.py, translation tables, indexes and test-database hygiene. - **tdc-design-enforcer**: Design-specification author for The Dreamer's Cave (tdcweb). - **tdc-documentation-expert**: Documentation writer for The Dreamer's Cave. - **tdc-performance-expert**: Performance specialist for The Dreamer's Cave — the Nuxt 4 rendering cost model (prerender, ISR via swr, SSR, client-only routes), client bundle and animation cost, Flask request and query cost on mysql-connector, and pool sizing. - **tdd-green-team**: Worker agent that implements the minimum production code to make failing tests pass. - **tdd-impl-runner**: TDD 中后期"业务代码实现"专职 agent。当代码骨架（含 TODO 占位）已经存在，并且用户希望**按需求 / 设计文档把骨架里的 TODO 填成真正可运行的业务实现**时，主动触发本 subagent。典型触发语包括："实现业务逻辑 / 填充 TODO / 补全代码 / generate implementation / implement skeleton / 实现接口 / TDD 实现阶段 / 骨架实现 / 把 TODO 填完 / 按需求实现代码 / 接着骨架把代码写完"。本 agent 的唯一职责是调用 `tdd-impl-generator` skill 完成业务代码实现，**不会重新生成骨架、不会写单元测试，也不会调用任何其他 skill**。支持 Java、C++、Scala、Python、Go、Rust、JavaScript、TypeScript 八种语言，能够识别同目录下已存在的 `*-tdd-impl-tasks.md` 进度文件以断点续作；并强制执行"TODO 零残留"硬约束。具备启动子 agent 的能力，仅用于 skill 内部 batch 并行派发。 ([wangbinquan/skills](https://github.com/wangbinquan/skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tdd-impl-runner.md) · ★2)
